try{
function sendPurchaseDataSS() {
    var revenueSS;
    var shippingSS;
    var orderIDSS;
    var uniqueProducts;
    var totalProducts = 0;
    var productsInfoSS = "";
    for (var i = 0; i < window.dataLayer.length; i++) {
        if (window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce && window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce.purchase) {
            var purchaseDataSS = window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce.purchase && window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce.purchase.actionField;
            var productDataSS = window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce.purchase && window.dataLayer[i].ecommerce.purchase.products;
            revenueSS = purchaseDataSS.revenue;
            shippingSS = purchaseDataSS.shipping;
            orderIDSS = purchaseDataSS.id;
            uniqueProducts = productDataSS.length;
            for (var j = 0; j < productDataSS.length; j++) {
                productsInfoSS = productsInfoSS + productDataSS[j].skuId + "=" + productDataSS[j].quantity + "--";
                totalProducts += productDataSS[j].quantity;
            }
            productsInfoSS = productsInfoSS.slice(0, productsInfoSS.length - 2);
            console.log(revenueSS, shippingSS, orderIDSS, uniqueProducts, totalProducts, productsInfoSS);
            break;
        }
    }
    window.SS && SS.Require && SS.Require(function() {
        SS.EventTrack.rpSync("revenueSS", revenueSS);
        SS.EventTrack.rpSync("uniqueProducts", uniqueProducts);
        SS.EventTrack.rpSync("totalProducts", totalProducts);
    });
}

window.SS && SS.Require && SS.Require(function() {

    });
});
